Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on models structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 926. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 246 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to models theory, we decompose the problem into 926 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 246. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
